Report: `Inn Trouble - ASSEMBLED - FORMATTED_Greek_PROOFREAD.md`. Decisions: `PROOFREAD_DECISIONS2.md` → section `Inn Trouble - ASSEMBLED - FORMATTED_Greek (2026-10-27)`.

Method: python-docx/lxml, `<w:t>` nodes only. Every block matched an exact substring occurring exactly once in the paragraph's current text (verify-before-write); changed characters were mapped onto the existing runs with a minimal diff (no run collapsing, no `set_para_text`); an edit spanning runs of different formatting would have been refused (none was). Paragraph count unchanged (1776 → 1776).
